====================================================================================================
64K A-B OVERLAP ANALYSIS (SUPERBPE)
====================================================================================================

Legend:
  A = Chinese superBPE
  B = Pinyin-Toneless superBPE

Method:
  • Only the 64K tokenizer pair is analyzed.
  • Forward mapping follows A→B conversion before target-vocab lookup.
  • Converter used in this run: pypinyin.Style.NORMAL(strict=False)
  • N对1 pair count = number of B tokens that are each mapped from N A tokens.
  • source_token_count = N × pair_count.
  • Plot Y scale mode: log

Files:
  • Output directory: table2_ab_overlap_superBPE_outputs
  • Report: table2_ab_overlap_superBPE_report.txt
  • Summary CSV: table2_ab_overlap_superBPE_summary.csv
  • Details CSV: table2_ab_overlap_superBPE_details.csv
  • Plot: table2_ab_overlap_superBPE_plot.png
  • Vector plot: table2_ab_overlap_superBPE_plot.svg, table2_ab_overlap_superBPE_plot.pdf
